# Skills · P · part 117

904 entries, prism-implement to privacy-by-design-checklist. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **prism-implement**: Implements a feature, fix, or task on the current branch against its plan, then ships it (commit, push, draft PR). Reads the plan and the routed architect docs before editing and updates the plan afterward. Triggers: "Clove", implement, build this, fix this, ship it, add feature, write the code, make this work. ([HunterMcGrew/PRISM](https://github.com/HunterMcGrew/PRISM/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/prism-implement) · 2 copies)
- **prism-init**: Initialize the .prism/ directory structure in the current project. Creates shared folders for brainstorms, research, plans, validation, designs, assets, and more. Use when starting a new project with Prism or when the user says "init prism", "set up prism", "initialize prism", "create .prism folder". ([TheDigitalGriot/prism](https://github.com/TheDigitalGriot/prism/tree/HEAD/.prism/shared/evals/v4.13.0-snapshot/skills/prism-init) · ★2 · 14 copies)

- **prism-instance-edit**: Safely edit PrismLauncher config (instance.cfg, prismlauncher.cfg) - checks the launcher is closed, backs up, edits, and re-reads to confirm the change survived. Use whenever editing a PrismLauncher instance's JVM args, memory, Java path, or other launcher settings. Complements mc-work, which decides what to change. ([Ven0m0/Win](https://github.com/Ven0m0/Win/tree/HEAD/user/.dotfiles/config/claude/skills/prism-instance-edit) · ★2)
- **prism-instance-edit**: Safely edit PrismLauncher config (instance.cfg, prismlauncher.cfg) - checks the launcher is closed, backs up, edits, and re-reads to confirm the change survived. Use whenever editing a PrismLauncher instance's JVM args, memory, Java path, or other launcher settings. Complements mc-jvm-tune, which decides what to change. ([Ven0m0/Win](https://github.com/Ven0m0/Win/tree/HEAD/user/.dotfiles/config/opencode/skills/prism-instance-edit) · ★2)
- **prism-intent**: Mira, the intent stage. Runs a grilling interview, one question at a time with a recommended answer on each, and writes intent.md into the ticket's plan folder for a person to approve. Use when starting new work, or when a plan folder has no approved intent. The Proposed outcome and the acceptance criteria replace user stories. Triggers: "Mira", write the intent, start an intent, grill me on this, define the scope, what are the requirements, write user stories, flesh out the requirements. ([HunterMcGrew/PRISM](https://github.com/HunterMcGrew/PRISM/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/prism-intent) · 2 copies)
- **prism-iterate**: Iteration phase combining plan updates and re-implementation. Use when plan needs adjustment after partial implementation or validation feedback. Triggers on "iterate on plan", "update and continue", "adjust the approach", or when validation reveals issues requiring plan changes. ([TheDigitalGriot/prism](https://github.com/TheDigitalGriot/prism/tree/HEAD/.prism/shared/evals/v4.13.0-snapshot/skills/prism-iterate) · ★2 · 18 copies)
- **prism-knowledge-intake**: Absorb knowledge from a book, article or other outside source the user is reading into PRISM-INSIGHT so that it fits the north star and stays searchable for future sessions. Use when the user shares book pages or photos, quotes an investing author, or says things like "책 읽은 거 올릴게", "인사이트 축적해줘", "이 책 내용 우리 로직에 녹여봐", "예전에 읽은 책에서 뭐라고 했지". Records source summaries and knowledge cards under docs/knowledge/; never changes trading rules directly (use prism-trading-change-review for that). ([dragon1086/prism-insight](https://github.com/dragon1086/prism-insight/tree/HEAD/skills/prism-knowledge-intake) · ★813)
- **prism-legal**: Drafts terms of service, reviews privacy policies, and assists with contract review, writing to the `## Legal & Compliance` section of `.prism/business/strategy.md`. Every output carries a "not legal advice" disclaimer and recommends licensed counsel when jurisdiction or product context is missing. Triggers: "Lex", terms of service, ToS, privacy policy, contract review, compliance, legal. ([HunterMcGrew/PRISM](https://github.com/HunterMcGrew/PRISM/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/prism-legal) · 2 copies)
- **prism-lifecycle**: Route Prism work to the host-native story or epic lifecycle and, when explicitly requested, advance a deterministic snapshot of all open Prism epics. Use when the user runs $prism:lifecycle, /prism:lifecycle, or /prism-lifecycle; asks to run or advance the primary host workflow; resumes one host Story or Epic; or asks to process all open Prism epics. Do not use for explicit Prism Callee requests. ([baldaworks/prism](https://github.com/baldaworks/prism/tree/HEAD/plugins/prism/prefixed-skills/prism-lifecycle) · ★17)
- **prism-light-layer**: Builds or selects the blurred prism-light layer for Prism graphics and backgrounds using the blurred-shape construction rules. Use for background patterns and for the light half of focus or distinguishing graphics. ([soofi-xyz/soofi-xyz-team-kit](https://github.com/soofi-xyz/soofi-xyz-team-kit/tree/HEAD/skills/prism-light-layer) · ★4)
- **prism-maintain**: Maintain the Prism codebase: measure cyclomatic complexity, refactor hotspots, and enforce the anti-slop type discipline. Use when the user asks about "complexity", "refactor", "hotspot", "maintainability", "code health", "anti-slop", "lint rules", or "clean up" the Prism repo. ([lza6/Armor-piercing-system-prompts](https://github.com/lza6/Armor-piercing-system-prompts/tree/HEAD/05-%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD%E4%B8%8E%E4%BB%A3%E7%90%86%E5%BC%80%E5%8F%91/prism-maintain) · ★14)
- **prism-market-research**: Produces competitive teardowns, TAM and segment sizing, and ICP research, and writes them to the `## Research` section of `.prism/business/strategy.md`. Uses the `deep-research` host capability when present. Triggers: "Kora", market research, competitive teardown, TAM, segment sizing, ICP research, market sizing. ([HunterMcGrew/PRISM](https://github.com/HunterMcGrew/PRISM/tree/HEAD/.cursor/skills/prism-market-research) · 2 copies)
- **prism-marketing**: Produces positioning, messaging hierarchies, campaign briefs, and content briefs, and runs SEO as a mode of content strategy, writing to the `## Marketing` section of `.prism/business/strategy.md`. Uses the `brand-voice` host capability when present. Triggers: "Charlie", positioning, messaging, SEO, marketing strategy. ([HunterMcGrew/PRISM](https://github.com/HunterMcGrew/PRISM/tree/HEAD/.cursor/skills/prism-marketing) · 2 copies)
- **prism-metrics**: Produces funnel analysis, cohort analysis, and dashboard specs, and writes measured outcomes to the `## Metrics` section of `.prism/business/strategy.md`, closing the loop back to `prism-strategy`. Uses the `xlsx` host capability when present. Triggers: "Tess", metrics, funnel analysis, cohort analysis, dashboard, KPI, conversion, retention. ([HunterMcGrew/PRISM](https://github.com/HunterMcGrew/PRISM/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/prism-metrics) · 2 copies)
